<h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/DgKy_JioK1F">Looking Back Wednesday 👀 Today&#x27;s prompt is things that look back at you - eyes and faces hiding in the everyday. My hero is a Jeep headlight doing a very good impression of an angry eye. Show me ONE photo of something staring back: headlights, windows, a knot in the wood, a face in the clouds. <h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/DgIOPVlGyZo">Pattern Tuesday 🔷 Patterns hide in plain sight - today&#x27;s prompt is repetition, anywhere you find it. My hero is a staircase at night, railings and treads repeating all the way up. Show me ONE photo of a pattern: tiles, fences, bricks, fabric, a row of anything at all.
